module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);

	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);

	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

	move_ready:[0..1] init 1;
	crashed:[0..1] init 0;

	[east]	(move_ready = 1) -> ( 1-3*p ):(x' = min (x+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = min (y+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = max (y-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(x' = max (x-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0);
	[west]	(move_ready = 1) -> p:(x' = min (x+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = min (y+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = max (y-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0) + ( 1-3*p ):(x' = max (x-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0);
	[north]	(move_ready = 1) -> p:(x' = min (x+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + ( 1-3*p ):(y' = min (y+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = max (y-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(x' = max (x-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0);
	[south]	(move_ready = 1) -> p:(x' = min (x+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = min (y+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + ( 1-3*p ):(y' = max (y-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(x' = max (x-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0);
	[check]	(move_ready = 0) & hasCrashed -> (crashed' = 1) & (move_ready' = 1);
	[check]	(move_ready = 0) & !hasCrashed -> (move_ready' = 1);
endmodule

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule 

rewards "cost"
	[east] true :1;
	[west] true :1;
	[north] true :1;
	[south] true :1;
	[update] true :5;
endrewards

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

	[west]	(move_ready = 1) -> p:(x' = min (x+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = min (y+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = max (y-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0) + ( 1-3*p ):(x' = max (x-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0);
	[north]	(move_ready = 1) -> p:(x' = min (x+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + ( 1-3*p ):(y' = min (y+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = max (y-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(x' = max (x-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0);
	[south]	(move_ready = 1) -> p:(x' = min (x+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = min (y+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + ( 1-3*p ):(y' = max (y-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(x' = max (x-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0);
	[check]	(move_ready = 0) & hasCrashed -> (crashed' = 1) & (move_ready' = 1);
	[check]	(move_ready = 0) & !hasCrashed -> (move_ready' = 1);
endmodule

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule 

rewards "cost"
	[east] true :1;
	[west] true :1;
	[north] true :1;
	[south] true :1;
	[update] true :5;
endrewards

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

	[west] true :1;
	[north] true :1;
	[south] true :1;
	[update] true :5;
endrewards

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

const int ystart = 0;
const int xtarget = 9;
const int ytarget = 9;
const double p = 0.01;

formula hasCrashed = ( 1=0 )|( x=0&y=7 )|( x=1&y=2 )|( x=1&y=3 )|( x=1&y=6 )|( x=2&y=2 )|( x=2&y=4 )|( x=2&y=6 )|( x=3&y=6 )|( x=3&y=9 )|( x=4&y=3 )|( x=4&y=5 )|( x=5&y=1 )|( x=5&y=2 )|( x=5&y=4 )|( x=5&y=5 )|( x=5&y=6 )|( x=5&y=8 )|( x=7&y=1 )|( x=7&y=2 )|( x=7&y=4 )|( x=7&y=6 )|( x=8&y=8 )|( x=9&y=1 )|( x=9&y=4 )|( x=9&y=7 );

module Robot
	x:[0..N] init xstart;
	y:[0..N] init ystart;
	move_ready:[0..1] init 1;
	crashed:[0..1] init 0;

	[east]	(move_ready = 1) -> ( 1-3*p ):(x' = min (x+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = min (y+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = max (y-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(x' = max (x-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0);
	[west]	(move_ready = 1) -> p:(x' = min (x+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = min (y+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = max (y-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0) + ( 1-3*p ):(x' = max (x-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0);
	[north]	(move_ready = 1) -> p:(x' = min (x+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + ( 1-3*p ):(y' = min (y+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = max (y-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(x' = max (x-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0);
	[south]	(move_ready = 1) -> p:(x' = min (x+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = min (y+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + ( 1-3*p ):(y' = max (y-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(x' = max (x-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0);
	[check]	(move_ready = 0) & hasCrashed -> (crashed' = 1) & (move_ready' = 1);
	[check]	(move_ready = 0) & !hasCrashed -> (move_ready' = 1);
endmodule 


module Adaptation_MAPE_controller

	[east]	(xhat = 0) & (yhat = 0) -> true;
	[south]	(xhat = 0) & (yhat = 1) -> true;
	[south]	(xhat = 0) & (yhat = 2) -> true;
	[south]	(xhat = 0) & (yhat = 3) -> true;
	[south]	(xhat = 0) & (yhat = 4) -> true;

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule 

rewards "cost"
	[east] true :1;
	[west] true :1;
	[north] true :1;
	[south] true :1;
	[update] true :5;
endrewards

	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

endmodule

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);

	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

	move_ready:[0..1] init 1;
	crashed:[0..1] init 0;

	[east]	(move_ready = 1) -> ( 1-3*p ):(x' = min (x+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = min (y+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = max (y-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(x' = max (x-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0);
	[west]	(move_ready = 1) -> p:(x' = min (x+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = min (y+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = max (y-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0) + ( 1-3*p ):(x' = max (x-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0);
	[north]	(move_ready = 1) -> p:(x' = min (x+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + ( 1-3*p ):(y' = min (y+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = max (y-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(x' = max (x-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0);
	[south]	(move_ready = 1) -> p:(x' = min (x+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = min (y+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + ( 1-3*p ):(y' = max (y-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(x' = max (x-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0);
	[check]	(move_ready = 0) & hasCrashed -> (crashed' = 1) & (move_ready' = 1);
	[check]	(move_ready = 0) & !hasCrashed -> (move_ready' = 1);
endmodule

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule 

rewards "cost"
	[east] true :1;
	[west] true :1;
	[north] true :1;
	[south] true :1;
	[update] true :5;
endrewards

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

	[west]	(move_ready = 1) -> p:(x' = min (x+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = min (y+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = max (y-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0) + ( 1-3*p ):(x' = max (x-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0);
	[north]	(move_ready = 1) -> p:(x' = min (x+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + ( 1-3*p ):(y' = min (y+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = max (y-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(x' = max (x-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0);
	[south]	(move_ready = 1) -> p:(x' = min (x+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(y' = min (y+1,N)) & (move_ready' = 0) + ( 1-3*p ):(y' = max (y-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0) + p:(x' = max (x-1,0)) & (move_ready' = 0);
	[check]	(move_ready = 0) & hasCrashed -> (crashed' = 1) & (move_ready' = 1);
	[check]	(move_ready = 0) & !hasCrashed -> (move_ready' = 1);
endmodule

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule 

rewards "cost"
	[east] true :1;
	[west] true :1;
	[north] true :1;
	[south] true :1;
	[update] true :5;
endrewards

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);

	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);

	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

	[east] true :1;
	[west] true :1;
	[north] true :1;
	[south] true :1;
	[update] true :5;
endrewards

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

module Knowledge
	xhat:[0..N] init xstart;
	yhat:[0..N] init ystart;
	step:[1..20] init 1;
	ready:[0..1] init 1;

	[west]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = max (xhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[east]	ready = 1 -> (xhat' = min (xhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[south]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = max (yhat-1,0)) & (ready' = 0);
	[north]	ready = 1 -> (yhat' = min (yhat+1,N)) & (ready' = 0);
	[update]	step >= c&ready=0 -> (xhat' = x) & (yhat' = y) & (step' = 1) & (ready' = 1);
	[skip_update]	step < c&ready=0 -> (ready' = 1) & (step' = step+1);
endmodule

module Turn
	t:[0..2] init 0;

	[east]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[west]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[north]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[south]	(t = 0) -> (t' = 1);
	[URC]	(t = 1) -> (t' = 2);
	[update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
	[skip_update]	(t = 2) -> (t' = 0);
endmodule

	[east] true :1;
	[west] true :1;
	[north] true :1;
	[south] true :1;
	[update] true :5;
endrewards
